# Codex handoff

## Current task

- Redesign the main workspace on local branch `DesignALL` as a line-separated, card-free, high-density three-column developer tool while preserving all existing behavior.
- Status: D1 implementation, gates, signed debug build, launch, and self-smoke are complete. The signed `DesignALL` app is running as PID `72469`; user visual acceptance is the only remaining D1 gate, and D2 has not started.

## Working area

- Current branch/worktree: `DesignALL` in `/tmp/deppy-sf06-integration`. Uncommitted files in this phase are the DesignALL specification and this handoff only. No design production code, manifest, lockfile, build artifact, or running app has been changed.
- Confirmed safeguards: PTY output/input queues, runtime command/event queues, terminal scrollback/cache classes, persisted restore size, remote frames, log bytes, exited backends, and shutdown joins are bounded; no Arc cycle or unbounded terminal scrollback leak was found statically.
- Confirmed gaps after second-pass review: warm/hidden replay retains some lifecycle events without a hard cap; web-push session jobs are admitted without a cap before slow HTTP delivery; archived-row rebinding mutates persistence before archive validation completes; exported `RemoteRuntimeClient` has no bounded TCP connect deadline or missed-heartbeat deadline; the process-global Git label cache never evicts unique paths; session log/archive GC scans have depth/byte but no entry-count budget.
- Rejected prior finding: valid persisted cwd is restored. `persist::load_workspace_restore_bounded` joins `sessions.cwd` into `PaneState.cwd` before `restore_pane`; the stability wave adds regression evidence only and does not change cwd production behavior unless that exact test fails.
- Orca's reusable design is architectural rather than a drop-in dependency: per-target generation-fenced connection ownership, bounded reconnect backoff, SSH keepalive plus relay sequence/ACK liveness, sleep/App Nap wake probing, remote relay-owned PTYs, durable leases/incarnation IDs, and explicit detach versus dispose.

11. Completed — write and self-review the four-checkpoint implementation plan.
12. Pending — implement, build, and review D1 through D4.

- 2026-07-23 runaway-protection program (A1, B1-B3, C1-C4) landed on branch `fix/child-rss-footprint`, 8 commits. Policy: warn-only, all freeze/kill actions are user-triggered. A1 `1ae9f6c`: session child RSS via per-pid `proc_pid_rusage` phys_footprint (memoized, ps-RSS fallback, never zero) — fixes the 263GiB shared-page double-count; same-uid other-pid lookup empirically verified without privileges. B1 `e96cbd7`: pure `process_storm` state machine (≥300 procs sustained 6s, hysteresis; CPU excluded to avoid build false-positives) + runtime "empty ps capture ≠ resolved" fix (retains last emitted). C1 `61cda08`: `dispatch2` `DISPATCH_SOURCE_TYPE_MEMORYPRESSURE` subscription (no polling). C2 `e61deb3`: `EmergencyPersistFlush` command → `flush_async_writes()` on pressure escalation (closes the only OOM loss window — DbWriteWorker's 50ms debounce batch, since Drop never runs on SIGKILL). B2 `1281f10`: storm banner + Activity badge + one-shot OS notification via `platform::notify` (NOT notify-rust — 2026-07-05 abort precedent). C3 `8f8f827`: heaviest-session pressure notification. B3 `8b47d67`: freeze(SIGSTOP)/resume(SIGCONT)/kill(reuses KillSession) buttons; `FreezeSession`/`ResumeSession` commands + `SessionFreezeChanged` event appended to wire tails (postcard-compatible, order C2→B3). C4: `비상_플러시는_drop_없이_배치를_커밋한다` proves Drop-independent durability; guarantee/non-guarantee scope documented. All commits pass fmt/clippy/check-boundary/check-deps/i18n-check; pty freeze test needs `--test-threads=1` (parallel run exhausts ptys — env limit, not a logic failure). Roadmap: `docs/runaway-protection-roadmap.md`. NOT yet merged to main; `scripts/package-macos.sh` has an uncommitted local strip-on-package tweak (size reduction) still pending a decision.

- 2026-07-22 the second Windows PTY review found a remaining single-cancel race: dropping the input sender does not discard already queued `sync_channel` messages, so a writer can enter a new `WriteFile` immediately after `CancelSynchronousIo` and make the pre-master join block. The accepted portable-pty-compatible order is now discard output, terminate/reap child, close master/HPCON while the reader drains (closing the ConPTY input peer), then cancel/join the writer and finally the reader. The PTY lane is applying this order to kill/drop/failure paths and updating source-law evidence; no timed cancel loop, detached closer, or hidden reaper is allowed. The first Windows follow-up's 27/27 macOS evidence is superseded until this final ordering freezes.

- 2026-07-22 PTY Windows red-team follow-up corrected the ConPTY teardown order. Local portable-pty 0.9 source declares `Inner { con, readable, writable, size }`, so stable field destruction invokes `ClosePseudoConsole` before closing the original readable pipe; cancelling the reader before master drop could therefore deadlock on pre-Windows 11 24H2 while ClosePseudoConsole waited for output drain. Windows now starts the cloned reader worker before attaching a child, switches the bounded output queue into zero-retention discard/drain mode at shutdown, cancels and joins the writer, terminates/reaps the child, drops the master/HPCON while the reader continues draining, and only then cancels and joins the reader. Child and writer spawn failures use the same drain-through-close order; reader-thread spawn failure happens before child attachment. Unix duplicated-fd/self-pipe teardown is unchanged. A deterministic full-capacity discard test and cfg-order source law were added. Agent and independent root verification both pass PTY 27/27 and session 50/50 with doc suites green; combined all-target check, strict Clippy, scoped Rust 2024 fmt, and diff-check pass. Windows target std was unavailable locally, so CI must still confirm Windows compilation/runtime. The failed first Windows design cancelled both workers only after dropping master; read-only review caught that ClosePseudoConsole can block before cancellation is reached, and it was replaced rather than retained. No detached close thread or reaper was introduced.

All shell/agent/split/restore paths enforce the 256-session cap before secret resolution, ID advance, subprocess spawn, or persistence; an invalid queued agent with a valid correlation emits one static failure plus `AgentSpawnResolved(None)`, while an invalid correlation is synchronously rejected before enqueue. Focused command 7/7, host 4/4, queue/session/bypass/pressure tests pass; full runtime plus doc-tests pass 183/183, `cargo check -p runtime --all-targets`, strict `cargo clippy -p runtime --all-targets -- -D warnings`, Rust 2024 rustfmt check, and global `git diff --check` pass. An earlier intermediate check was blocked by the then-active PTY lane's missing receiver type, and passed once that sibling froze; no workaround or debt was added.
- 2026-07-22 PTY independent red-team reopened the otherwise-green lane for a Windows teardown blocker. `stop_worker_io` dropped the portable-pty ConPTY master before reaching `CancelSynchronousIo`; portable-pty 0.9's private `Inner` stores `PsuedoCon` before its original readable pipe, so master drop calls `ClosePseudoConsole` before that pipe closes. On pre-Windows-11-24H2 behavior this can block waiting for output drain and prevent child kill/cancel/join forever. The PTY lane is implementing a Windows-specific discard-and-drain sequence: cancel/join the writer, keep the reader draining without retaining queue chunks while the HPCON closes, then cancel/join the reader; Unix self-pipe teardown must remain unchanged. No detached drop thread/reaper is permitted, and macOS green tests are not treated as Windows evidence.

ConnectorCoordinator's `connector-{counter}` IDs restart at 1 while audit operation IDs are globally unique, so C must inject or generate a process-unique boot prefix and prove two coordinator lifetimes cannot collide. The durable preflight proof/grant must also bind a private, non-logged digest of the exact validated input bytes so audited arguments A cannot authorize called arguments B; service call construction and any proxy hook/forwarder handoff must consume and verify server/tool/input together. C's current shared-tree edit is intentionally not buildable yet and root identified a transient double `finish_operation_slot` underflow plus stale field/constructor references; these were reported before any root test run.
- 2026-07-22: AU01 shared audit/storage API reached its first green milestone: audit 45/45 and storage 79/79 (124 total). Plans bind operation/server/tool/exact decision/live schema to one transactional permission+audit preflight; only Prepared yields a non-Clone call grant and Denied yields a non-callable receipt. All preflights require a <=32 KiB JSON object, raw encrypted audit remains NULL by default, preview uses key/pattern redaction, and raw-input `AuditRecord`/`ToolApprovalRequest` are non-Clone with the owned legacy request zeroized on drop. One initial storage compile failure was a legacy test reading a newly private `AuditOperation` field and was corrected to its accessor. Root/red-team still require an actually <=500-character ellipsis implementation, explicit delivery-Unknown transition/reuse tests, typed outcome consumption in the service port, and removal of any fabricable public plan-decision path before the API freezes; proxy and service executors remain in progress.
- 2026-07-22: AU01 root/red-team review froze additional safety gates before integration. Tool arguments must be an object and within 32 KiB before schema/permission/approval/audit/call; the durable grant must be unforgeable, bind the exact operation/server/tool/decision/live-schema preflight rather than only id+lifecycle, and be consumed by value for one external call. Proxy startup reconciles prior Prepared rows before accepting calls; one internal operation ID spans policy/preflight/call/outcome; pending Ask input is bounded, zeroized, and dropped on cancel/stale/timeout/double resolve; pre-call cancellation closes Prepared as Failed, delivery uncertainty closes it as Unknown, and post-call outcome-write failure must remain non-retryable. Apple 공식 Mac 도움말(2026-07-20 확인)은 거부 후 `System Settings → Privacy & Security → Files & Folders`에서 앱별 접근을 변경하도록 안내한다. 또한 `마지막 출력을 에이전트로`는 `LastOutputIntent::SendToAgent`, 추출 응답 주입, 대상/프리셋 메뉴, 번역 키와 테스트를 제거하고 copy-only pending set으로 단순화한다. 선택 텍스트의 일반 `에이전트로 보내기`와 `마지막 출력 복사`는 유지한다.

App은 active `terminal_sessions`와 warm `WorkspaceUi.session_entries`를 map으로 전달하며 warm의 과거 focused flag는 제거한다. inactive session은 편집/context menu 없이 표시하고 클릭 시 workspace id+tab+pane을 담은 FocusSession을 보내 App이 switch 후 focus한다. 새 kittest는 A→B 포커스 이동 후 두 트리 유지, 활성 A 재클릭 때 A만 명시 접힘, 열린 inactive B session 클릭의 정확한 focus target을 검증한다. 다음은 fmt/compile/집중 테스트다.

UI 취소가 generation만 폐기하고 callback listener는 180초 timeout까지 남긴 것이 자기충돌 원인이었다. auth callback에 atomic 취소 경로를 추가하고 ConnectorsUi가 취소 시 신호를 보낸 뒤 listener를 즉시 drop하도록 했으며, 종료 race에는 50ms×20회 bounded bind 재시도만 허용했다. Slack은 DCR 미지원, 고정 app ID, Marketplace 게시/내부 앱만 허용하므로 `http://localhost:47456/callback` 고정 PKCE flow와 설정 안내를 추가했다. 일반 OAuth는 기존 127.0.0.1+fallback 경로를 유지한다. 새 auth/Slack/UI 회귀를 포함해 검증을 다시 실행한다.

- 2026-07-16: GitHub verification found the exact upstream root cause and a proposed fix. Alacritty issues `#6942` (Korean composition followed by dropped punctuation/ASCII) and `#8079` (one Korean-IME Space produces two spaces) remain open and are labeled macOS/winit dependency defects. winit PR `#4478` (`macOS: Fix Korean composing text commit key handling`) explicitly fixes both: macOS may call `insertText` twice inside one `interpretKeyEvents`; winit 0.30.13 leaves `marked_text` stale until after the whole call, so the second callback sees composition still active and can emit a duplicate Commit, while a `Committed` early return drops the triggering ASCII command. The PR clears marked text immediately, forwards post-commit trigger characters as regular key input, and removes the overbroad early return. Local inspection confirms the unpatched code exists verbatim in cached winit 0.30.13 (`src/platform_impl/macos/view.rs:410-430`). The PR is still open with one small 12-addition/8-deletion commit, no reviews/comments, and targets winit 0.31.0-beta.2; the latest stable and this app's resolved dependency are both 0.30.13, whose March release does not contain it. Recommended implementation is a reproducible minimal backport into a locally patched/vendored 0.30.13 rather than pointing eframe at the incompatible 0.31 beta. Once source-level events are corrected, reassess/remove the overlapping punctuation key-up heuristic to avoid compensating twice. The egui 0.33 focus changes (`#7827` and `Context::text_edit_focused`) are already included in this app's egui 0.35, so the Agents→PTY focus defect remains local focus arbitration rather than a missing egui upgrade.

The configured MB total is divided across the current active+warm resident runtime count, rebroadcast after switches/evictions/deletions, and each worker now accepts shares below the UI's 32MiB global minimum down to a defensive 1MiB. Focused app division and runtime clamp regressions pass. Implemented conservative 30-minute idle-shell suspension: it is eligible only when every live session is explicitly a shell, no agent is detected, each sampled process group contains exactly one shell leader, CPU is at most 1%, and no sample/spawn is pending; agents, unknown sessions, child jobs, busy/unsampled shells remain protected. Focused auto-suspend/live-tracker tests pass.
- 2026-07-16: Removed Glow from ordinary production dependency features and added app feature `render-glow` for the existing A/B harness. Shared shutdown logic now supports eframe's feature-dependent `on_exit` signature. The render benchmark script explicitly builds with `render-glow` (and combines it with `bench-alloc` when requested). Offline `cargo check -p deppy-sijo` passes both with default production features and with `--features render-glow`; dependency-tree inspection shows Glow/Glutin only in the feature-enabled tree. `git diff --check` passes.
- 2026-07-16: Combined offline regression run passed completely: app 250 passed/2 ignored, runtime 117 passed, and i18n 6 passed, including locale key completeness and all new memory-policy regressions. Unlike earlier sandbox runs, the current environment also allowed the process/network-dependent app/runtime tests in these targets to complete without failures. Next verification is strict Clippy plus a default-feature production release/link inspection.
- 2026-07-16: `bash -n scripts/render-bench.sh` and strict offline Clippy passed for default app+runtime all targets and for the `render-glow` app configuration (retaining only the pre-existing explicitly allowed `while_immutable_condition`). The optimized default release build passed. The prior bundle executable was 38,075,312 bytes and linked both OpenGL and Metal; the new Wgpu-only release is 37,953,760 bytes (121,552 bytes smaller), no longer links `OpenGL.framework`, and retains AppKit/QuartzCore/Metal as expected. Proceeding to official packaging/signature verification.

- 2026-07-16: Implemented Activity summary PID deduplication and explicit app-versus-child RSS accounting. The summary now counts each in-process app PID/CPU sample once while retaining each session child-tree aggregate, and localized detail text reports the split. The first focused test compile stopped on a test-fixture-only type mismatch (`process_group` was written as `i32` instead of its declared `u32`); corrected before rerunning. `cargo test -p deppy-sijo ui::activity::tests --offline --no-fail-fast` then passed 9/9, including the new duplicate-PID regression. No runtime code path was implicated by the initial fixture failure.

- 2026-07-16: Remediation started after the user approved proceeding. Before changing renderer configuration, dependency source inspection found that `eframe::NativeOptions::default()` already sets Wgpu `SurfaceConfig::LOW_LATENCY` on native builds; the earlier audit had looked only at `egui_wgpu::WgpuConfiguration::default()` and therefore overstated the drawable-buffering opportunity. Metal latency tuning was removed from the implementation plan. Production still unnecessarily enables Glow for the renderer benchmark, so only that feature separation remains in renderer scope.

- 2026-07-16: Follow-up memory review corrected the earlier causal overstatement. The old debug executable's +34.5% measurement is only isolated `pre_run_native` RSS; macOS RSS includes resident clean mappings, while Activity Monitor's app-memory/footprint view is centered on dirty and compressed memory, so file size, in-app RSS, and Activity Monitor are not directly interchangeable. A same-state full-GUI `footprint`/`vmmap` comparison was attempted but blocked because the sandboxed native process exited before inspection and the tools reported permission/process-lifetime failures. Confirmed two product issues: `activity_summary` adds the same whole-app PID RSS once per active/warm runtime, and the 128MB "global" terminal cache budget is broadcast unchanged to each runtime and enforced only against that runtime's sessions (three resident runtimes can therefore retain roughly 384MB of estimated terminal cache before per-runtime archival pressure). Ordinary non-exited shells also mark a warm workspace live, bypassing `max_warm` and 30-minute eviction until the RAM-derived `max_live_warm` limit of 3–8. These latter two can increase real memory when restored sessions/scrollback are populated; Activity duplication only inflates the displayed total. Dependency inspection confirms the app links only `wgpu 29.0.4`, not multiple wgpu versions. Production nevertheless enables both Wgpu and Glow for benchmark switching, and egui-wgpu defaults to Metal high-throughput surface buffering; those are lower-confidence optimization targets, not established causes of the cross-Mac 50% report. No product source was modified.

- 2026-07-16: Diagnosed the reported other-Mac 50% memory increase. The root `Deppy-Sijo-0.1.0-arm64.zip` accepted earlier by Gmail embeds a 110,034,880-byte development/debug `deppy-sijo`, whereas `target/bundle/Deppy-Sijo-0.1.0-arm64.zip` embeds the 38,075,312-byte optimized release binary. Three isolated self-RSS samples per artifact at `pre_run_native` averaged 23.06MiB for the old package and 17.15MiB for release, making the old package 34.5% higher before renderer/UI allocation. The Activity summary has a separate accounting defect: every in-process workspace monitor reports `std::process::id()` and whole-process RSS, while `activity_summary` sums every workspace snapshot and then all session-child RSS, so two live workspaces can duplicate the app RSS and agent processes intentionally expand the displayed boundary. No product source was modified in this diagnostic turn. Current signed bundle executable SHA-256 is `3da61c6dab9fb9add274708a10634b753d6301b2dcc3282bb4475b36b70d6a55`; old debug bundle executable SHA-256 is `96be224ef0a02eaa855c4265e7a291cec2982637be62cd25c1eeaadc4e36cbc4`.

- 2026-07-16: Investigated another-Mac terminal output rendering Korean as literal `???`. Both D2Coding faces are compiled with `include_bytes!` into `deppy-sijo`, so the terminal does not depend on AppleGothic being installed and the app bundle is not missing a terminal font resource. Reproduced the portability boundary locally: a macOS zsh started with a Finder-like empty environment reports `US-ASCII`, while setting `LANG=en_US.UTF-8` reports `UTF-8`. The PTY backend currently sets terminal capability variables but no locale fallback, so GUI launch environment differences can make shell/agent programs replace Korean with question marks before rendering.
- 2026-07-16: First implementation attempt used the small `sys-locale` crate, but adding a new direct package required a crates.io index update and the restricted build environment could not resolve `index.crates.io`; the test command was stopped. Replaced that dependency with the already-linked `objc2-foundation` `NSLocale` API, retaining the same system-locale behavior without introducing a new package or network requirement.
- 2026-07-16: The first offline PTY compile with the Foundation implementation caught a missing tail return in `macos_utf8_locale`; corrected the expression before rerunning tests. No binary or bundle was produced from that failed compile.
- 2026-07-16: Implemented the locale fallback at the `PortablePtyBackend` boundary so it covers ordinary shells and directly spawned agents. On macOS, an empty/inherited-missing `LANG` is populated from Foundation `NSLocale`, normalized to a supported UTF-8 POSIX locale with `en_US.UTF-8` as the final fallback; explicit user `LANG` and all `LC_*` settings remain untouched. `cargo test -p pty --offline --no-fail-fast` passed 21/21, including a real child `locale charmap` assertion under an empty `LANG`, explicit-locale preservation, and BCP-47 normalization tests.
- 2026-07-16: Integration verification passed after the locale fix: `cargo test -p terminal -p deppy-sijo --offline --no-fail-fast` completed with app 246 passed/2 ignored and terminal 60 passed; `cargo clippy -p pty --all-targets --offline -- -D warnings` and `cargo build -p deppy-sijo --offline` also passed. Proceeding to a clean release app bundle rather than shipping the larger debug executable used for local iteration.
- 2026-07-16: Cached the resolved macOS UTF-8 locale in a process-wide `OnceLock`, so Foundation lookup and libc locale validation happen only for the first PTY rather than on every shell/agent spawn. This avoids repeated Objective-C conversion work while retaining per-command explicit `LANG` checks.
- 2026-07-16: Final post-cache verification passed: PTY tests 21/21, strict PTY Clippy, and the official offline release package build. `target/bundle/Deppy Sijo.app` is a 42MB arm64 release bundle; deep/strict code-signature verification passed. ZIP SHA-256: `351180194196fd96ec6f3a1ae979f720f779c6880bf92de1ce862c87942d4317`.
- 2026-07-16: Pre-commit scope confirmed for the two completed terminal portability fixes: AppKit image-only Command+V capture/deduplication plus macOS PTY UTF-8 locale fallback, their dependency lock entry, and this handoff. Unrelated design `dist` changes, local `.codex`, logs, and the older root-level ZIP remain unstaged.
- 2026-07-16: Investigated intermittent image clipboard paste requiring a second Command+V. Current egui-winit consumes a recognized paste key-down after attempting a text-only clipboard read; image-only pasteboards therefore produce neither `Event::Paste` nor the original pressed `Event::Key`. The local workspace compensated by detecting only the later V key-up, leaving the first gesture vulnerable to focus/modifier loss. OpenAI Codex #3397/#10523 and Ghostty discussion #10478 report the same macOS image-only Cmd+V gap; Ghostty PR #11571 proposed the same PNG/TIFF-to-temp-path conversion already present locally, while Claude Code's changelog confirms direct Cmd+V mapping as the successful trigger-side fix.
- 2026-07-16: Extended `native_key_monitor` to retain app-local Command+V key-downs as a bounded/fresh clipboard gesture while continuing to return every `NSEvent` unchanged. Matching prefers layout-aware `charactersIgnoringModifiers == v` with ANSI/Korean physical V fallback, and rejects Control/Option/Function chords. `WorkspaceUi` now starts file/image detection from that native key-down and uses the former egui V key-up only as fallback; a 600ms gesture record removes the delayed release so one physical paste cannot duplicate text or images.
- 2026-07-16: Focused verification after the image-paste trigger change passed: `cargo test -p deppy-sijo native_key_monitor --no-fail-fast` (4/4), `cargo test -p deppy-sijo ui::workspace::tests --no-fail-fast` (35/35), `cargo test -p terminal --no-fail-fast` (60/60), `cargo check -p deppy-sijo`, scoped rustfmt, and `git diff --check`. No unrelated design build artifacts or local `.codex` files were modified.

Therefore a native non-mock table component in this app would require a new structured agent-session transport and UI, not a PTY renderer change. The current root `AGENTS.md` policy remains the correct low-risk path for table-first *terminal text*.

Printable punctuation arrives from egui-winit as paired `Key` + `Text` events; the mapper now has a regression test proving `.`, `,`, `/`, `?`, `!`, and space write exactly once. More importantly, pane click/search-close/focus-cycle now records an optimistic `pending_focus` input target before the async runtime `MuxUpdated` response, so the first printable key cannot be dropped or routed to the prior pane. A pending terminal refocus also overrides one-frame stale hidden `TextEdit` focus, while visible popups/windows still block terminal input. - 2026-07-15: Fixed the remaining Korean IME special-character gap. During an active Korean composition, macOS/egui can emit a printable `Key` plus `Ime::Commit` but omit `Text`; the terminal mapper previously ignored the Key to prevent normal Key/Text duplication, so `.` and other symbols were lost on their first press. Added a restricted ASCII fallback for punctuation, shifted symbols, digits, and space only while an IME batch is involved. Any matching Text/Commit removes exactly one fallback candidate, so normal input and repeated symbols remain single-entry; Ctrl/Command/Option shortcuts are never synthesized. Added regression coverage for missing-Text, normal Key/Text, and repeated punctuation. Verified `cargo test -p terminal input_mapper::tests` (13 passed), `cargo test -p deppy-sijo ui::workspace::tests` (22 passed), `cargo build -p deppy-sijo`, scoped `rustfmt` for `input_mapper.rs`, and `git diff --check`. - 2026-07-15: The user confirmed the first `.` after a Korean jamo was still lost. Root cause was deeper than the prior same-frame fallback: winit 0.30 macOS intentionally suppresses a printable key-down when `interpretKeyEvents` commits marked text, then emits only that key's key-up after IME state returns to Ground. Added a short-lived, session-bound pending IME release state. An IME Commit arms it; the next printable key-up restores the swallowed ASCII character. Commit/Text and same-frame fallback characters are tracked so an already-delivered symbol is never duplicated; pure modifier releases are ignored so Shift+symbols remain recoverable, while other non-printable releases consume the state. Regression tests cover `ㅁ` + swallowed Period key-down + Period key-up, Commit already containing `.`, same-frame fallback deduplication, and modifier-release ordering. Verified `cargo test -p terminal` (59 passed), `cargo test -p deppy-sijo ui::workspace::tests` (25 passed), `cargo check -p deppy-sijo`, `cargo build -p deppy-sijo`, and `git diff --check`.

The current key-up fallback therefore matches the observed mechanism but remains sensitive to later focus/frame state. - 2026-07-15: The user found the inverse failure after the missing-key recovery: a second consecutive space can expand to two spaces, and one comma can occasionally become two. Alacritty #8079 provides the exact macOS/winit event sequence: one physical Space produces both `Ime::Commit(" ")` and a normal `KeyboardInput` whose egui translation includes `Text(" ")`. The current workspace maps both text-bearing events, while its native monitor only deduplicates fallback bytes, so the existing test never exercised the real duplicate path. Remaining plan changed to reconcile Commit/Text/fallback output against one physical-key cardinality ledger, add exact upstream-sequence regressions for one/two spaces and comma, then rerun the full Rust verification and refresh the signed app bundle.
- 2026-07-15: Replaced the independent native/egui fallback filters with one ordered `ImeTextReconciliation` pass. AppKit key-downs and egui printable Key events are treated as duplicate observations of the same physical keys; the reconciler caps combined `Ime::Commit` + `Text` output to that cardinality, restores only unclaimed keys for IME batches, and inserts a missing earlier key before a later delivered key so rapid punctuation cannot reorder. The key-up safety net now consumes the reconciled (not raw duplicate) text ledger. `cargo test -p deppy-sijo ui::workspace::tests --no-fail-fast` passes 35/35, including exact Alacritty #8079 single-space, two physical spaces, comma Commit+Text, Hangul+comma, non-IME comma duplication, missing punctuation, and rapid `.` then `,` ordering cases. Remaining plan: inspect/format the scoped diff, run terminal/full app tests, clippy/check/build, then refresh/sign/launch the bundle.

Additional verification passes: native AppKit monitor tests 3/3, all terminal tests 59/59, and `cargo check -p deppy-sijo`. Remaining plan: full app test, strict clippy with the documented unrelated allow, build, bundle refresh/sign/launch, and manual physical IME confirmation.

Only manual physical Korean IME confirmation remains (`한글` then Space twice, Hangul then comma once/repeated, `ㅁ.`, and rapid `.,`).
- 2026-07-16: Dependency/API audit remediation began. The terminal renderer now requests egui focus in the same frame that logical terminal keyboard ownership becomes active, emits `PlatformOutput::ime` only when `Memory::owns_ime_events(response.id)` is true, and the workspace rechecks that ownership after overlays before consuming input events. Existing AppKit/winit Korean cardinality reconciliation remains intact. Scoped rustfmt passed; terminal renderer tests pass 17/17 and workspace input tests pass 35/35. Next: pin Rust/MSRV, add dependency-audit CI, then stage low-risk dependency upgrades.
- 2026-07-16: Build reproducibility/security automation added. `rust-toolchain.toml` and every workspace package now pin/inherit Rust 1.96.1; `cargo metadata` confirms all 20 packages report that `rust-version`. Added weekly/PR RustSec `audit-check`, cargo-deny license/source/duplicate checks, weekly Cargo and monthly GitHub Actions Dependabot updates, and a `deny.toml` license policy based on the actual lockfile license expressions. YAML parsing, offline Cargo metadata, and `git diff --check` pass. Local cargo-deny/audit execution is unavailable because those binaries are not installed; CI uses their official maintained actions. Next: stage TOML and native menu dependency upgrades independently.

Added an adversarial 50k-character TOML operator regression test for the upstream parser hardening; all 18 config tests pass. - 2026-07-16: Began the release-RAM investigation. Five fixed-duration current-release launches succeeded far enough to remain alive, but external `/usr/bin/time -l`, `ps`, and `pgrep` could not report RSS because the sandbox denies the required sysctl/process-list services. Added a benchmark-only macOS self-RSS fallback using the already-available `proc_pidinfo(PROC_PIDTASKINFO).pti_resident_size`; it avoids spawning/depending on `ps` for app RSS while retaining `ps` only for optional child-tree aggregation. Next: test this sampler, capture current release stages, then build/capture the pre-remediation baseline identically.
- 2026-07-16: The new self-RSS test passes. Added a `pre_run_native` stage and captured five isolated WGPU release runs for current and baseline `169a888`. Baseline pre-GUI RSS was 18,137,088–18,153,472 bytes; current was 18,137,088–18,268,160 bytes, overlapping within 0–114,688 bytes. Mach-O used sections increased only about 19KB. Therefore the IME state (bounded 64-key queue) and TOML/muda upgrades do not explain the reported MB-scale runtime increase.
- 2026-07-16: Found and fixed the material font-memory waste. On macOS the default UI font and CJK fallback both point to `/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/AppleGothic.ttf` (15,255,648 bytes), but `install_cjk_fallback` read/stored it twice under `cjk` and `ui`. `App::new` also initialized font-change snapshots to sentinels, rebuilding the full definitions again on the first frame even though main had already installed the same settings. The definitions now keep one cached `Arc<FontData>` and use `cjk` as the proportional primary when paths match; repeated definitions reuse the same Arc, and App snapshots start from the installed config. Guaranteed raw-font reduction is 15,255,648 bytes steady-state and at least 45,766,944 bytes of avoided first-frame duplicate allocation/ownership pressure. Font sharing tests (3/3), the startup snapshot regression test (1/1), RSS sampler test, and `git diff --check` pass. Next: run IME/app regressions, clippy/release package/sign, then record the final bundle result.

Strict app clippy passed with only the previously documented unrelated `while_immutable_condition` allow, release build passed, and `git diff --check` is clean. ## 2026-07-26 Codex usage data connection

- Connected the top-bar Codex usage display to the official app-server `account/rateLimits/read` RPC.
- Added 60-second rate-limit refresh and mapped primary/secondary windows to the `5h`/`wk` percentages and progress bar.
- Claude usage remains unavailable because the installed Claude CLI exposes no supported subscription-usage query source; its placeholder is retained.
- `cargo build -p deppy-sijo --bin deppy-sijo` completed successfully.
- Re-signed and launched `target/bundle/Deppy Sijo.app` with the configured Developer ID; running PID was `53476`.
- Tests, clippy, and i18n gates were not run because no commit was requested.

## 2026-07-26 Orca-compatible provider usage

- Root cause for the empty Codex usage display: `account/rateLimits/read` returns `{ rateLimits: { primary, secondary } }`, while the first parser expected the windows at the result root and accepted integers only.
- Codex now unwraps `rateLimits`, accepts floating-point `usedPercent`, and classifies 5-hour/weekly windows using `windowDurationMins` with the primary/secondary fallback retained.
- Claude now reuses the existing Deppy status-line proxy. Claude Code `rate_limits.five_hour` and `rate_limits.seven_day` percentages are written as a non-secret local snapshot after a live response and read by the top bar with a two-second cache. No Claude Keychain credential read was added.
- Reference: `stablyai/orca` `codex-fetcher.ts` and `claude-statusline-rate-limits.ts`.
- The first combined build command failed only because the proxy package is named `mcp-proxy`, not `deppy-mcp-proxy`. The corrected app+proxy build completed successfully.
- Replaced and Developer-ID-signed both binaries in `target/bundle/Deppy Sijo.app`; launched PID `78159`.
- Tests, clippy, and i18n gates were not run because no commit was requested.

## 2026-07-26 Claude PTY fallback and compact workspace header

- Added `crates/app/src/claude_usage.rs`: when no live status-line snapshot exists, a bounded background PTY starts the real Claude CLI, sends `/usage`, parses current-session and weekly percentages, and refreshes every 60 seconds.
- The probe uses the existing `pty` abstraction, a 25-second timeout, a 100 KB output cap, a 2-second startup delay, and a 2-second render-settle window. Deppy does not read Claude Keychain credentials directly.
- Fixed the parser to continue scanning up to 12 lines after each usage heading instead of returning on the first non-percent line.
- Sidebar active-session status color is now `#c84d4d`.
- Workspace header heights changed from `48.0/39.1` to `43.2/35.19`; avatar and initial font changed from `24/12` to `21.6/10.8`.
- App and proxy debug build succeeded. Developer-ID-signed bundle launched as PID `90868`.
- User requested the usage and latest sidebar UI changes be separated into PRs; commit/PR gates and branch split remain.
## 2026-07-26 Claude usage fallback and compact active-session styling

- Added a hidden Claude PTY `/usage` fallback so the top bar can obtain current-session and weekly usage without reading macOS Keychain credentials directly. The live statusline snapshot remains the primary source.
- Added the active session rail color `#c84d4d`, reduced workspace header height by 10%, and reduced the workspace avatar by 10%.
- Built and launched the Developer ID-signed app bundle; the running process is PID 90868.
- Commit gates passed: `cargo test -p deppy-sijo --bins file_tree`, `cargo clippy -p deppy-sijo --bin deppy-sijo -p mcp-proxy --bin deppy-mcp-proxy -- -D warnings`, and `cargo test -p i18n`.
- The delivery plan is two stacked PRs: usage/repository integration first, followed by the isolated active-session and compact-header visual change.
## 2026-07-26 stacked PR delivery

- PR #37 (`workspaceDesign-usage` -> `workspaceDesign`): repository context and Codex/Claude usage, including the hidden Claude `/usage` PTY fallback.
- PR #38 (`workspaceDesign-active-session` -> `workspaceDesign-usage`): active session rail `#c84d4d` and 10% smaller workspace header/avatar.
## 2026-07-26 workspace accent interaction follow-up

- Restored session rails to the shared agent-state color palette; removed the fixed active `#c84d4d` override.
- Inset session hover and focused backgrounds now derive from the parent workspace avatar accent (`0.16` hover, `0.24` focused); workspace headers remain untinted.
- Next: build/relaunch first, then directly inspect whether the Claude usage probe renders in the top bar.
## 2026-07-26 Claude usage direct verification

- Direct PTY `/usage` verification succeeded: Claude returned `Current session 7% used` and `Current week (all models) 13% used`.
- The app still renders `— 5h · — wk`; this is not an authentication or Keychain failure.
- Root cause: Claude's TUI positions text with ANSI cursor movement, and the current stripper collapses labels to `Currentsession` / `Currentweek`, while the parser expects whitespace-separated labels.
- Required follow-up: normalize labels for matching (for example, compare lowercase alphanumeric-only forms) while retaining percentage extraction from following lines.
- Fixed Claude usage label matching by comparing lowercase alphanumeric-only forms, which accepts TUI-collapsed `Currentsession` and `Currentweek` labels.
- Reduced workspace header height by another 6px (`43.2 -> 37.2` with repository subtitle, `35.19 -> 29.19` without).
- Provider progress bars now fill from the larger of 5h/week usage so a nonzero weekly value remains visible when 5h is zero.
- Claude usage parsing now prefers the last TUI redraw instead of an initial temporary 0% frame.
- Reduced inset-session rail left padding from 4px to 0px.
- Reduced the fixed top-provider usage allocation by 30px (`260 -> 230`).
- The Claude probe now stops sending automatic Enter once the usage panel is detected, allowing the initial 0% frame to update to the stable account value before parsing.
- Moved Claude/Codex usage from the top bar to the left side of the bottom status bar.
- Removed the bottom status bar's connection-health label and workspace-count label; session/MCP/service/memory indicators remain.
- Disabled the SidePanel's full-height separator and repainted it only above the 26px bottom status bar, so the bottom usage/status region remains visually continuous across the window.
- Replaced the bottom usage provider text labels with compact vector Claude/Codex marks.
- Codex usage now uses OpenAI green `#10a37f` for both its mark and progress fill instead of the weak gray text color.
- Matched the supplied provider marks: cyan three-ellipse Codex knot and orange eight-ray Claude starburst.
- Provider usage now reuses `paint_announcement_provider_logo` from the Home announcement list instead of maintaining a second icon implementation; Codex progress uses the same theme hyperlink color as the Home knot.
- Reduced the bottom status-bar Claude/Codex logo allocation and actual vector geometry by 30% (`18px -> 12.6px`) while preserving the Home announcement logos at 22px.
- Git workspace subtitles now show only the branch because the former "repository" segment was derived from and always duplicated the workspace folder name; non-Git workspaces remain one-line folder names.
- Set bottom provider logos to 13.5px and Home announcement logos to an actual scaled 20px.
- Moved inset-session rails another 2px left (`0 -> -2`) and added a foreground mask that removes the built-in SidePanel resize line from the bottom status bar while restoring its horizontal top border.
- Corrected the inset-session alignment: restored the rail-local inset to `0` and reduced the whole session-row outer indent from `20px` to `18px`, so only the space left of the rail shrinks while the rail-to-text gap remains unchanged.
- Increased bottom Claude/Codex provider icons from `13.5px` to `14.5px`; Home announcement icons remain `20px`.
- Removed the foreground separator mask and disabled egui's built-in full-height SidePanel resizer. Sidebar width is now controlled by a custom 6px drag handle whose interaction and separator both end at the top of the 26px bottom status bar.
- Created branch `colorRail` with the existing workspace-design changes intact. Replaced the inset session's full-height status rail with a centered fixed `4x4px` square marker while retaining the existing status/pulse color rule and a fixed 2px marker-to-text gap.
- Removed the uncommitted `colorRail` branch and returned to `workspaceDesign`; the 4x4 marker experiment was reverted to the existing full-height status rail. Reduced non-agent two-line session rows from `38px` to `34px` and moved both active/inactive session rows left by reducing their outer indent from `18px` to `16px`.
- Refined the workspace/session dimensions: Git two-line workspace headers are now `34px`, their visual left inset is `4px`, avatars are `21px`, and the pre-avatar inset is `5.8px`. Non-agent two-line session rows are `36px`; `2.25px` vertical text margins yield an `8px` computed inter-line gap. Session rails are fixed `36px` high and use `2px` normal / `4.5px` attention widths with a matching `2..4.5px` pulse.
- Reduced fixed session rail height from `36px` to `35px`, removed the explicit `2px` maximum-rail-to-text gap, and reduced the post-session external bottom spacing from `4px` to `2.5px` for active and inactive workspace groups.
- Added a vector Git-branch icon before workspace branch subtitles. Session text now starts after the rail's actual current width, eliminating the residual gap left by reserving the 4.5px attention width for normal 2px rails.
- Rebuilt bottom provider usage as icon, colored 5h percentage, 5h progress, `5h`, separator, `이번 주`, and colored weekly percentage; added a provider separator and normalized bottom status text styles and explicit usage labels to 13px.
- Restored inset-session text to the fixed maximum-rail alignment so pulse/attention width changes do not move text. Focus/hover fill now starts immediately after the rail's actual current width, and the workspace Git-branch icon was reduced by 30% (`10.5px -> 7.35px`).
- Workspace headers now show an avatar-accent hover fill at `0.16` opacity whether their session list is collapsed or expanded; the effect is limited to the header response and does not tint the inset-session area.
- Replaced the workspace branch line icon with a fixed square `4x4px` marker. Extended only the workspace hover fill by `1px` on the right, leaving status-count and disclosure geometry unchanged.
- Reduced the workspace branch marker to `3x3px` and vertically centered it against the second-line branch galley instead of aligning it to the line's top edge.
- 2026-07-27: Reviewed the proposed document-editor architecture against the clean `main` worktree and saved the corrected plan in `docs/document-editor-development-plan.md`. Key decisions: keep the runtime terminal mux unchanged and put the document surface in an App-owned outer split; add a separate bounded `DocumentIoIntent` lane; start with egui 0.35 native `TextEdit` and a bounded `String` instead of directly depending on the egui-0.34 `egui_code_editor` line or introducing Rope; preserve Markdown by treating source text as authoritative; implement local storage first; and defer Deppy remote file RPC/SFTP to a separate milestone because the current remote protocol is terminal transport, not a remote filesystem. This turn changed documentation only and intentionally ran no build or tests.
- 2026-07-27: Reframed document-editor Milestone 0 as three evidence-driven spikes rather than a preselected `TextEdit` implementation. Completed work: defined (1) native/App-WebView integration-boundary checks, (2) a common egui `TextEdit` versus embedded Monaco bakeoff with 1 MiB acceptance and 10 MiB stress fixtures, and (3) a Markdown authoring/fidelity comparison that determines whether direct-rendered editing needs TipTap/ProseMirror alongside Monaco. Modified files: `docs/document-editor-development-plan.md` and `docs/CODEX_HANDOFF.md`. Key decisions: keep runtime terminal pane identity unchanged; freeze performance budgets before measurement; preserve source with byte-identical no-op round trips and range-local edits; choose the editor/state/IPC design only after results. Tests: no code build or test was run because this phase changed documentation only; `git diff --check` passed with no diagnostics. Failed approaches: none in this phase. Remaining work: implement the three compile-only spikes, capture target-hardware metrics, write the decision record, then select parser/highlighter and freeze contracts. Next commands: `git diff --check`; `git diff -- docs/document-editor-development-plan.md docs/CODEX_HANDOFF.md`; `rg -n \"struct App|impl App\" crates/app/src/app.rs`; `rg -n \"WorkspaceUi|CentralPanel|SidePanel\" crates/app/src/app.rs crates/app/src/ui`.
- 2026-07-27: Added the document-editor reference synthesis requested after reviewing current Warp and Orca implementations. Current objective: implement Milestone 0 against a product profile that combines Warp's native Rust terminal, separated content/selection/render models, delta/anchor edits, viewport rendering, bounded incremental language work, and shared buffers with Orca's familiar Monaco-class source editing, tabs/search/diff/comment-to-agent flow, local/SSH parity, feature-specific fallbacks, lazy expensive-surface lifecycle, and resource attribution. Completed work: added dedicated Warp strengths, Orca strengths, combined Deppy architecture/rules, Milestone 0 scoring criteria, and reference-project entries; changed the schematic `DocumentState` to a technology-neutral `DocumentContentState` so Monaco's `ITextModel` and Rust cannot both become full-source authorities. Modified files: `docs/document-editor-development-plan.md` and `docs/CODEX_HANDOFF.md`. Key decisions: preserve the existing native terminal mux; do not require a webview for terminal startup; let the selected editor own interactive edit/IME/undo state; keep Rust authoritative for path security, revisions, persistence, remote transport, and sensitive-content policy; preserve Markdown source via range-local edits; consider a custom native rope/tree editor only if both `TextEdit` and Monaco miss their budgets. Tests: no code build or test was run because this phase changed documentation only; `git diff --check` passed with no diagnostics. Failed approach: the first focused `rg` validation used backticks inside a double-quoted shell argument, causing shell command substitution and printing the installed `orca` CLI help; it changed no file, and the safely quoted rerun succeeded. Remaining work: freeze quantitative Milestone 0 budgets, implement the three compile-only spikes, capture target-hardware results, and select the editor/parser/highlighter/IPC contracts. Next commands: `git diff --check`; `sed -n '127,260p' docs/document-editor-development-plan.md`; `sed -n '400,445p' docs/document-editor-development-plan.md`; `rg -n \"struct App|impl App\" crates/app/src/app.rs`; `rg -n \"WorkspaceUi|CentralPanel|SidePanel\" crates/app/src/app.rs crates/app/src/ui`.
